## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
Cookies Details

# 2026 Webex Customer Awards Nominations Now Open!
On May 21, 2026Jun 4, 2026By [Aruna Ravichandran](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/arunaravichandran-2/)5 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/2026-webex-customer-awards-nominations-now-open) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/2026-webex-customer-awards-nominations-now-open&title=2026%20Webex%20Customer%20Awards%20Nominations%20Now%20Open!) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/2026-webex-customer-awards-nominations-now-open)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/2026-webex-customer-awards-nominations-now-open/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/2026-webex-customer-awards-nominations-now-open/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/webex-customer-awards.jpg)
We’re excited to announce that nominations for the 5th Annual [Webex Customer Awards](https://www.webexone.com/awards.html) are officially open!
This is your chance to showcase your organization’s achievements, celebrate breakthrough innovation, and highlight how you’re using Webex and Cisco technology to transform collaboration and customer experiences.
Each year, the Webex Customer Awards honor the teams and organizations pushing boundaries, transforming the way people work, and creating meaningful impact with technology. And this year, we’re bringing the celebration to **WebexOne 2026 in Austin, Texas** , where we’ll recognize the best of the best in true Webex style.
In previous years, we celebrated our customers in style with a glamorous ceremony:
This year, we’re rolling out the red carpet once again — Austin style. Whether you bring your ballgown or your boots, we want to celebrate your success, your innovation, and the business transformation you’re driving with Webex.
## Celebrate Your Success and Gain Industry Recognition
The [Webex Customer Awards](https://www.webexone.com/awards.html) are designed to spotlight organizations that are leading with innovation and delivering measurable impact. Finalists will be recognized during a red-carpet celebration at WebexOne 2026, taking place October 5–8 at the Fairmont Austin in Austin, Texas, with opportunities to be featured across Webex blogs, customer stories, and marketing channels. It’s a chance to elevate your organization’s story, strengthen your industry visibility, and showcase how you’re shaping the future of AI-powered collaboration and customer experience.
## Showcase Innovation and Real-World Impact 
We are seeking organizations that are harnessing the power of Webex and Cisco technologies to reimagine collaboration, elevate employee and customer experiences, and drive innovation. Submissions that highlight measurable outcomes and real-world impact will receive top consideration. Whether you’re transforming hybrid work, enhancing customer journeys, or pioneering AI innovations, we want to hear your story!
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%20961%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
## **Spotlight Category**
Last year, we introduced the **People’s Choice awards** , where WebexOne attendees got to cast their votes for the most exciting and inspirational stories. We’re bringing back the **Dynamic Duo: People’s Choice** award, honoring organizations and Cisco Partners for their innovative and strategic use of Webex solutions that inspire others.
## Explore Diverse Segments Celebrating Collaboration Excellence
We are thrilled to announce that this year’s [Webex Customer Awards](https://www.webexone.com/awards.html) are bigger than ever, featuring 11 fabulous categories organized into four dynamic segments! We can’t wait to spotlight the incredible ways you are using Webex solutions. Ready to see where your success fits in?
**Pioneers:** **awards celebrating companies using Cisco AI to unlock new possibilities, simplify work, and shape smarter collaboration and customer experiences.**
  * **AI Innovator: Collaboration Devices & Webex Suite**


This award recognizes the company that has most successfully showcased vision, foresight, and innovation to elevate employee experiences with the [Cisco AI Assistant](https://www.webex.ai/) and agentic AI.
  * **AI Innovator: Customer Experience**


This award celebrates a company that has demonstrated vision, foresight, and innovation to elevate customer experiences with the [Cisco AI Assistant](https://www.webex.ai/) or [Webex AI Agent](https://www.webex.ai/).
**Powerhouses:** **awards recognizing companies using Cisco products in exceptional ways to connect teams, transform workspaces, elevate customer journeys, and build future-ready workplaces.**
  * **Collaboration Champion**


This award recognizes one company in each region (AMER, EMEAR, APJC) that has most successfully harnessed the power of the Webex Suite and its capabilities including [Meetings](https://www.webex.com/suite/meetings.html), [Calling](https://www.webex.com/suite/enterprise-cloud-calling.html), [Mess](https://www.webex.com/suite/messaging.html)[aging](https://www.webex.com/suite/messaging.html), [Vidcast](https://www.vidcast.io/), [Slido](https://www.slido.com/), [Webinars](https://www.webex.com/suite/webinar.html), and [Events](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/suite/events.html).
  * **Workspace Wizard**


This award honors the company that pushed the boundaries of workplace innovation through its bold and creative use of [Cisco devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html) and technology.
  * **Future-Proofed Workplaces Genius**


This award honors a company making the future-proofed workplace a reality by using Cisco products—such as [Networking](https://www.cisco.com/), [Meraki](https://meraki.cisco.com/), [Cisco Spaces](https://spaces.cisco.com/), Security, or [ThousandEyes](https://www.thousandeyes.com/)—alongside Cisco collaboration devices or [Webex](https://www.webex.com/) solutions.
  * **Customer Experience Extraordinaire**


This award honors the company that most effectively leverages [Webex Contact Center](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/contact-center.html) solutions to transform the customer journey and deliver exceptional customer experiences.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
**Game Changers:** **awards honoring organizations applying Webex and Cisco technology to reimagine industry experiences, solve real-world challenges, and create meaningful impact.**
  * **Bold Explorer: Healthcare**


This award honors the company that has demonstrated creativity and out-of-the-box thinking in its use of [Webex](https://www.webex.com/) products within the healthcare sector.
  * **Bold Explorer: Finance**


This award celebrates the company that has showcased creativity and out-of-the-box thinking in its use of [Webex](https://www.webex.com/) products in the financial sector.
  * **Bold Explorer: Government & Public Sector**


This award recognizes the company that has most creatively demonstrated out-of-the-box thinking in their use of [Webex](https://www.webex.com/) products in the government and public sector.
**All-Stars: awards celebrating the advocates, partners, and teams who bring Webex to life through standout moments, stronger connections, and experiences worth cheering for.**
  * **Webex Luminary**


This award celebrates our best Webex customers – individuals that advocate for Webex and are visionaries in their organization driving change by rallying others to embrace Webex.
  * **Dynamic Duo: People’s Choice**


This people’s choice award, voted on by WebexOne attendees, recognizes a Cisco Partner and Webex customer that collaborated on innovative implementations of [Webex](https://www.webex.com/) solutions.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
## Nominate Your Company or Your Customer Now
Step into the spotlight and let your innovation shine. Mark your calendars and [**submit your inspiring entries**](https://www.webexone.com/awards.html)**by July 10, 11:59 CT**. We can’t wait to see your inspiring award submissions and celebrate together at [WebexOne](https://webexone.com/) 2026 in October.
## Webex Customer Awards 2026: Quick Facts
**When is the deadline to submit a nomination?**
The deadline to submit nominations is Friday, July 10, at 11:59PM CT.
**Do I need to attend WebexOne 2026 to win?**
Yes, all award finalists must attend WebexOne 2026 in person to be eligible to win. Winners will receive a gift in person.
**Who can nominate a customer for a Webex Customer Awards Award?**
We encourage customers to self-nominate; in addition, Cisco employees and partners can submit a nomination on behalf of a customer.
**Is this program limited to a specific geographic region?**
The Webex Customer Awards is a global program, and we welcome submissions from all regions.
**When and where will the winners receive their awards?**
The winners will be publicly announced at WebexOne 2026 at the Fairmont Austin in Austin, Texas. The awards ceremony will be a live, in-person event at WebexOne 2026. Winners will receive their winners’ gifts in person. Stay tuned for the exact date and time of the ceremony.

# Building Tomorrow's Workplace Today: The Era of Connected Intelligence
On Feb 10, 2026Feb 10, 2026By [Snorre Kjesbu](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/snorrekjesbu/)7 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/building-tomorrows-workplace-today-era-connected-intelligence) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/building-tomorrows-workplace-today-era-connected-intelligence&title=Building%20Tomorrow%E2%80%99s%20Workplace%20Today:%20The%20Era%20of%20Connected%20...) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/building-tomorrows-workplace-today-era-connected-intelligence)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/building-tomorrows-workplace-today-era-connected-intelligence/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/building-tomorrows-workplace-today-era-connected-intelligence/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/building-tomorrows-workplace-today.jpg)
##### The Workplace Paradox
Here’s what keeps me up at night: we’re building workplaces for people who don’t work the way we think they do anymore.
I’ve spent the better part of three decades in technology, and I’ve never seen a moment quite like this. The office isn’t dead, but it’s not what it was. Remote work isn’t a pandemic relic; it’s a preference. And AI isn’t coming to transform work. It’s already here. It’s in our meetings, summarizing our calls, and quietly rewriting the rules of productivity. Yet for all this change, most organizations are still operating with frameworks built for a world that no longer exists.
That’s the disconnect. Companies are trying to solve yesterday’s problems with outdated thinking. They’re deploying AI without strategy, sprinkling it across products like seasoning without understanding the recipe. They’re investing in devices without understanding how spaces actually get used. They’re chasing innovation without asking the most important question:  _what are we actually trying to do?_
At [Cisco Live EMEA](https://www.ciscolive.com/emea.html), we’re not just showcasing products. We’re presenting a vision. One where Connected Intelligence becomes the operating system for how work happens. Where people and AI don’t compete, they each make the other better. Where physical and digital spaces blend seamlessly. Where customer experiences aren’t transactional, they’re transformational. 
This is about building the workplace of today for the workforce of tomorrow. And it starts with understanding that intelligence isn’t just in the cloud. It’s everywhere: at the edge, in the room, on the desk, in every conversation, and across every interaction.
## Intelligent Workspaces – Where Distance Becomes Zero
For years, we’ve talked about “bringing the remote participant into the room.” Noble goal. Wrong framing. The real challenge isn’t making remote people feel included. It’s making _every_ space intelligent enough to adapt to how people naturally collaborate, whether they’re across the table or across the world.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
This is what we call**Distance Zero:** the idea that intelligence starts at the edge, where devices capture people, space, and context in real time, so AI can improve the work experience as it happens. Not after the fact. Not in some cloud data center. Right there, in the moment, adapting to what’s unfolding. 
**The Room Kit Pro G2 and Desk Pro G2** : Cinematic Quality, Simplified. Walk into any executive boardroom and you’ll find a paradox: incredible technology that’s incredibly complicated. The **Cisco Room Kit Pro G2** changes that equation. This is a powerful room system for large rooms with multi-camera support, one-click setup, and AI-powered video intelligence that frames speakers dynamically. Designed to simplify large rooms and the most demanding AV scenarios with advanced multi-camera intelligence, scalable AVoIP room orchestration, single-click setup, NVIDIA-powered AI workflows, and seamless meetings on any platform. This is collaboration that feels effortless.

For individuals, the **Cisco Desk Pro G2** delivers that same intelligence at the desk –and now we are expanding that experience to the huddle space. With its dual-lens camera system, featuring a crystal-clear 4K 48MP lens and a dedicated 105-degree wide-angle lens, everyone in your huddle stays sharp and in frame. Pro-grade 4K display. One-cable docking. Precision whiteboarding. It transitions seamlessly from executive desks to huddle spaces, delivering elite performance wherever work happens. Plus, express installation means IT can deploy fast, and users can plug in and start immediately. 
And for frontline, and mobile workers, the **Cisco Wireless Phone 9821** provides secure, reliable communication that moves with them through hospitals, retail floors, and manufacturing plants. Runs on PhoneOS and designed for business calling, this phone operates over Wi-Fi and seamlessly integrates into existing communications workflows, enhancing efficiency and connectivity throughout the day. Because connected intelligence isn’t just for knowledge workers. It’s for everyone.

## One System, Managed Intelligently
Cisco wants to meet you where you work. Through integrating **Control Hub workspaces data with** Amazon Quick, Microsoft Copilot, our own AI Assistant and more, we’re bringing conversational AI-driven management directly into the tools you already use. For the first time, Workspace Designer allows you to visualize and configure your rooms in 3D with Cisco room devices and verified Samsung Smart Displays, IT and facilities teams can design, deploy, and optimize workspaces at scale with 3D visualization and verified interoperability. 
Intelligence at the edge. Devices that work as a unified system. Control at scale, without complexity. That’s Distance Zero made real.
## Connected Collaboration – Where Conversations Become Context 
Now let’s zoom out and talk about the system.
Most collaboration tools treat meetings, messaging, and content as separate experiences. You have a meeting. Then you Slack about the meeting. Then someone asks what was decided, and you spend 20 minutes digging through transcripts. Meanwhile, action items are scattered across sticky notes, chat messages, and handwritten notebooks.
**Connected Collaboration** is about creating an intelligent system where conversations carry context seamlessly across tools, teams, and time. Where AI doesn’t just transcribe, it understands intent, surfaces decisions, and moves work forward.
## Breaking Barriers, Building Connections
Our **Speech-to-Speech Translation** powered by the Translator Agent delivers real-time, human-sounding translation that enables natural conversations across languages. Not robotic playback. Fluid speech that preserves tone and builds trust. Imagine a team in Paris collaborating seamlessly with engineers in Bangalore and customer success in São Paulo, all speaking their native languages. That’s Connected Collaboration.
**Ask AI Assistant for Webex Calling** instantly surfaces relevant context from prior calls, meetings, and messages—and extends that context to enterprise applications through integrations with Amazon Q Index and Glean—so you can stay focused on the conversation, not on searching for information. And with **AI Assistant + Copilot Connector Integration** , we’re delivering seamless, bi-directional integration between Webex and Microsoft Copilot. One intelligent experience that respects how you actually work, not how vendors think you should.
Underpinning everything is **Zero Trust for Suite** , delivering enhanced end-to-end encryption for meetings and calls. Because connected intelligence only works if it’s built on trust.
**[**Connected Customer Experiences**](https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/ai-impact-delivering-customer-success-ai-powered-cx-innovations-operational-excellence-cisco-live-2026-amsterdam/) – elevating operational excellence with AI**
Let’s shift to customer experience, where AI reveals its transformational potential.
Contact centers have been broken for years. Long wait times. Repetitive questions. Agents buried in multiple screens. But what if AI could become an active teammate, handling routine interactions, routing intelligently, and empowering agents to focus on complex, human moments that require empathy and judgment?
That’s **the power of Connected Customer Experiences** : smart, personalized interactions built on trust, context, and human connections.
## Smarter Routing, Better Workforce Planning, Unified Experiences
**AI Routing** uses intelligent optimization based on context, caller history, intent, and agent availability to match customers to the best-suited agent, for every interaction. Better experiences. Faster resolutions. Happier customers.
**Native AI Forecasting and Scheduling that is intuitive** and purpose-built for the AI era, accurately predicting staffing needs by modeling real-time interaction data, AI agent deflections, and dynamic business scenarios. Simple. Intuitive. Accurate.
**Webex Contact Center for ServiceNow** embeds enterprise-grade contact center capabilities directly into ServiceNow, creating a unified experience where cases and interactions connect automatically. No context switching. No friction.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
And with Cisco **AI Assistant for on-premises (CCE)** , we’re bringing real-time assist, wrap-up summaries, and real-time transcriptions to on-prem deployments to empower both the agents and supervisors to deliver exceptional experiences. Modern on-premises experiences with AI innovations.
##### _Adaptive, Resilient Platform – The Foundation That Scales_
Innovation is exciting. But without a secure, scalable foundation, it’s just noise.
Our a**daptive, resilient platform** proactively secures, manages, and scales your business without adding complexity.
##### _Deploy, manage, and optimize from anywhere. That’s operational intelligence._
**Cisco AI Pods for Webex** enable secure, on-premises AI deployments with complete control over your data and compliance. [**Webex Calling Hybrid**](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/documents/pdf/webex-calling-hybrid-aag-cm-173302.pdf)lets you modernize communications while leveraging existing investments, migrating on your terms with Cloud PSTN and AI Receptionist capabilities.
We are also strengthening our commitment to digital sovereignty by expanding data residency for Webex Suite in the UK. By utilizing dedicated cloud locations, we will provide in-country data processing and storage for the Suite.
Our **continued investment in**[**Cisco UCM**](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/suite/enterprise-cloud-calling/CUCM.html)**and CMS** delivers the latest security updates, new government and security certifications, and AI features for on-premises deployments. And **Control Hub** provides centralized administration with real-time insights and AI-powered assistance across every environment.
## The Invitation
Connected Intelligence isn’t a product launch. It’s a philosophy. A recognition that the future of work will be defined by how intelligently our tools, spaces, and experiences adapt to us.
At Cisco Live EMEA, we’re showcasing what happens when devices become intelligent, collaboration becomes contextual, customer experiences become agentic, and platforms become adaptive. When technology stops being something, you manage and starts being something that empowers. 
This is the workplace of today, built for the workforce of tomorrow.
And this is just the beginning.
Let’s build it together.

# One Platform. Every Space. Zero Distance
On Feb 2, 2026Feb 9, 2026By [Espen Løberg](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/espenloberg/)8 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/one-platform-every-space-zero-distance) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/one-platform-every-space-zero-distance&title=One%20Platform.%20Every%20Space.%20Zero%20Distance) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/one-platform-every-space-zero-distance)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/one-platform-every-space-zero-distance/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/one-platform-every-space-zero-distance/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/ciscoroomkitprog2-dualptz.jpg)
  * **Cisco Room Kit Pro G2:** Replace complex AV patchworks with single AVoIP-first architecture
  * **Cisco Desk Pro G2:** Bring dual 4K cameras to any desk or huddle space for any platform
  * **Cisco Wireless Phone 9821:** Enterprise calling with AI features for frontline workers


At Cisco, collaboration devices aren’t standalone products—they’re the human interface of our complete workplace platform. When networking, security, and collaboration work together as one connected system, organizations can future-proof their workplaces at scale.
We’ve earned market leadership in intelligent collaboration devices by delivering on three principles: easy to use, easy to deploy, beautiful to experience. Our customers prove these principles work—managing thousands of meeting rooms with minimal IT staff while delivering consistent experiences end users love and actionable insights facilities teams depend on.
### That’s the power of One Cisco.
From the network fabric that powers AVoIP, to the zero-trust security that protects every endpoint, to Control Hub’s AI-driven management of millions of devices—our devices are part of something bigger. And when everything works as one system, complexity disappears.
Now we’re extending that platform advantage across every workspace—from complex conference rooms to individual desks to factory floors. Same principles. Same platform. Same consistency our customers already trust.
For every device we build, we ask the same question: Will this make connecting with another person feel more natural, or will it get in the way? That question drove us to leadership in meeting rooms. Today, it’s guiding us to solve the complexity that remains in high-impact spaces—powered by the only vendor with a complete, end-to-end workplace ecosystem.
## Cisco Room Kit Pro G2: The End of the AV Puzzle
The top challenge for IT is the complexity of building and maintaining high-impact spaces. Until now, large rooms required a fragile patchwork of DSPs, extenders, and switchers from multiple vendors.
### Cisco is solving this AV puzzle.
Meet the Cisco Room Kit Pro G2—replacing the complexity with a single, AVoIP-first, Agentic AI-first, simplification powerhouse.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
For more than a decade, we’ve collaborated with NVIDIA to bring intelligence to every meeting room. And now we’re partnering again to solve the AV puzzle.
We worked together to design a Cisco and NVIDIA Jetson compute module unlike anything seen before. This powerful collaboration device features NVIDIA’s industry-leading AI/ML engines, delivering 25 times the AI processing power of the previous generation. Real-time spatial awareness, enhanced noise removal, advanced people framing are all processed locally for security and speed. Just intelligence where it needs to be: in the room.
  * **One-click AVoIP:** 8PoE ports power and manage cameras, microphones, and controllers —no HDMI adaptors, XLR, or power injectors. Network-based audio, video, power, and control with seamless extensibility with Cisco 9200 series switches.
  * **Cinematic Meetings Intelligence:** Agentic Director supports up to 7 cameras (including Room Vision PTZ) and 8 microphones to enable advanced speaker tracking, cinematic cross-views, and flexible room reconfiguration—all natively supported, with HDMI display output up to 8K.
  * **Complete Visibility:** See everything in the room with Control Hub – including 3rd party displays, analog audio connections and HDMI cables. Workspace Advisor delivers recommendations for how to optimize the room experience.​
  * Designed for the Microsoft Device Ecosystem Platform (MDEP); supporting fully featured Webex, Teams, and Zoom meetings, plus interoperability with Google Meet. You can also Instantly connect via USB-C for BYOD and any meeting platform.
  * New lightweight, structurally reinforced chassis is 50% lighter with zero performance loss.


This is Distance Zero extended to your most complex spaces. Accelerated by NVIDIA. Delivered by Cisco. Orderable in February. Shipping Q2 CY26.
But high-stakes decisions don’t stop with your auditoriums, or training and conference rooms.
## Cisco Desk Pro G2: Bring Distance Zero to More Spaces
We’ve proven we can simplify collaboration at the desk and in large conference rooms. Now we’re bringing that same seamless experience to a new environment: small huddle spaces, where quick decisions and important conversations happen.
Meet the Cisco Desk Pro G2—one device that adapts to multiple workspace scenarios. For high-stakes one-on-ones, dual cameras deliver executive presence. When colleagues gather, it transforms into an instant collaboration space. Accelerated by NVIDIA AI for AI-powered intelligence wherever critical work happens.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
  * **Professional Video for Any Scenario:** The dual 4K camera system features a 48MP tele-lens for executive presence and a 105° wide-angle for small groups, with both lenses working simultaneously to create natural depth and perspective. Our Agentic Director automatically switches between Frames mode (where every participant gets their own window) and group overview mode ensuring the best view at any moment. The dual camera can also capture Cisco Spatial Meetings for consumption on Apple Vision Pro.​
  * **All-Day Display:** The 27-inch 4K screen delivers 2000:1 contrast ratio and 98% DCI-P3 color coverage for true-to-life visuals, engineered for reduced blue light and eye strain for comfortable, extended use.
  * **One Cable. One Button:** A single USB-C cable delivers 80W charging plus PC port replication—connecting a second 4K display, peripherals, and storage. One button joins any meeting: Webex, Teams, Zoom, or Google Meet.
  * **Built to Scale:** Zero Touch Provisioning via Control Hub enables instant, global deployment. Wi-Fi 6E and Deep Sleep modes provide energy efficiency, while high post-consumer recycled materials support your ESG goals.


The same platform powering more than a million devices worldwide, now extended to any small space. Available to order now.
We believe that [Distance Zero](https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/be-in-the-room-from-anywhere/) needs to reach every worker, including those who are always on the move.
## Cisco Wireless Phone 9821: Communication for the Frontline
Nurses, warehouse staff, manufacturing teams—they need reliable communication that moves with them through demanding environments. Consumer devices lack enterprise integration. Third-party solutions create management complexity. And when milliseconds matter for safety, connection delays are unacceptable.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
The Cisco Wireless Phone 9821 extends your collaboration infrastructure and enables frontline workers with the leading Agentic AI in Cisco´s calling platform.
  * **Enterprise Integration & Frontline Design: **Native integration with Unified Communications Manager (CUCM) and Webex Calling provides seamless platform extension. Shared device deployments are supported—ideal for shift workers. The rugged design with tactile keypad and swappable battery handles demanding frontline environments.
  * **When Milliseconds Matter:** Wi-Fi 6E delivers ultra-low latency and clear audio for critical, real-time communication in fast-paced environments—factories, warehouses, and healthcare facilities. AI-powered noise removal and integrated man-down detection enhance safety and clarity. The configurable action button and push-to-talk capability enable instant response.
  * **Managed at Scale & Built for Enterprise: **The phone runs on PhoneOS for consistent management with the 9800 Series. Deploy and manage via CUCM or Control Hub and get end-to-end assurance with Cisco Thousand Eyes. It’s secure, reliable, and customizable for enterprise needs.


Available to order in Q2 CY2026.
## One Platform, Complete Visibility
These devices share something that sets Cisco apart: they’re intelligent nodes on a unified platform. Deploy through the same Control Hub interface. Update automatically across your entire estate. Gain insights that optimize your workspace environment—all with security built into the network infrastructure.
When devices are native to the network, IT gains complete visibility across the entire workspace portfolio: device health, room utilization, network performance, security status, environmental conditions. All from a single interface.
This is the ecosystem advantage that sets Cisco apart. Others deliver hardware, but IT is left integrating disparate management systems and troubleshooting across disconnected platforms. With Cisco, collaboration devices, networking infrastructure, and security work as one seamless system.
These devices and this platform enable Distance Zero in any space. But visibility alone isn’t enough—you need intelligence that drives action.
## AI Agent Integrations: Workspace Intelligence in Your Workflow
We’ve proven we can deliver complete visibility through Control Hub. Now we’re extending that intelligence into new workflows enabled by your generative AI tools.
IT administrators shouldn’t context-switch between tools to understand workspace performance. That’s why we’re bringing Control Hub intelligence directly into the platforms you already use—Amazon Quick Suite, Microsoft Copilot, and the Cisco AI Assistant – with AI Agent Integrations.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%20977%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
Ask questions that matter: “Which spaces are underutilized in our London office?” or “Show me collaboration patterns across our east coast buildings.” AI analyzes your workspace portfolio and delivers actionable insights on utilization, patterns, capacity, and optimization. 
Unlike generic AI tools, these integrations tap directly into Control Hub’s real-time device data, utilization metrics, and environmental sensors—giving you answers grounded in actual workspace performance. Your data remains secure, maintaining the same enterprise-grade controls you already trust. 
The power comes from seeing the complete picture. Rather than managing individual rooms, you’re understanding organizational collaboration patterns, identifying opportunities to improve experiences, and making strategic decisions about where to invest next. From a single building to your entire global portfolio. 
Starting in Q1 2026, beta participants will be the first to transform workspace management through AI-powered insights in their existing workflow.
## Workspace Designer: Plan Once, Deploy with Confidence
Optimizing spaces over time requires intelligence. Getting devices into the right spaces from the start requires a plan. [Workspace Designer](https://cs.co/workspace-designer) eliminates deployment risk before equipment arrives. Create 3D visualizations of your meeting spaces with optimal camera placement and viewing angles. Walk through your space virtually, test sightlines, and see exactly how the room will function—all before ordering equipment.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
Now, thanks to our partnership with Samsung, select from [verified displays](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/7sw4gab/Cisco-collaboration-devices-certification-program)—LCD, large-format LED, or ultra-wide 21:9—that automatically configure resolution, color balance, and refresh rate the moment you enter the room. These aren’t arbitrary recommendations—they’re tested for seamless compatibility. 
Design once. Validate everything. Deploy with confidence. 
## Continuous Innovation, Consistent Principles
We’ve just introduced the most advanced collaboration devices we’ve ever built—accelerated by NVIDIA, managed through Control Hub, planned through Workspace Designer, optimized by AI. Behind every innovation, three principles remain constant: easy to use, easy to deploy, beautiful to experience.
That discipline is why customers manage millions of our devices through a single platform, trust us with their most critical spaces, and invest confidently knowing their infrastructure will adapt.
For every device we build, I ask the same question: Will this make connecting feel more natural, or will it get in the way? These innovations are our answer—where people and AI work side by side, where distance disappears, and where technology gets out of the way.
Because they’re part of **One Cisco** —connected to your network, secured at the edge, managed through a single platform—they’re intelligent nodes in a workplace that learns, adapts, and scales.
### Come see our innovations at [ISE 2026](https://www.webex.com/us/en/live-events/ise-2026.html) in Barcelona and [Cisco Live](https://www.ciscolive.com/emea.html) Amsterdam!

# Cisco named 2026 Global Enterprise Desktop Phone Company of the Year by Frost & Sullivan
On Aug 12, 2026Aug 12, 2026By [John Reeder](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/johnreeder/)5 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/cisco-named-2026-global-enterprise-desktop-phone-company-of-the-year-by-frost-sullivan) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/cisco-named-2026-global-enterprise-desktop-phone-company-of-the-year-by-frost-sullivan&title=Cisco%20named%202026%20Global%20Enterprise%20Desktop%20Phone%20Company%20of%20the%20...) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/cisco-named-2026-global-enterprise-desktop-phone-company-of-the-year-by-frost-sullivan)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/cisco-named-2026-global-enterprise-desktop-phone-company-of-the-year-by-frost-sullivan/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/cisco-named-2026-global-enterprise-desktop-phone-company-of-the-year-by-frost-sullivan/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/hero-phones.jpg)
Frost Sullivan has named Cisco the [2026 Global Enterprise Desktop Phone Company of the Year](https://explore.webex.com/frost-sullivan-gated/frost-and-sullivan-d?utm_campaign=8265%20utm_medium=website%20utm_source=wdc%20utm_term=download_report%20utm_content=frost_and_sullivan_gated_page%20utm_team=Integrated%20https://explore.webex.com/frost-sullivan-gated/frost-and-sullivan-d?utm_campaign=8265%20utm_medium=website%20utm_source=wdc_blogs%20utm_term=download_report%20utm_content=frost_and_sullivan_gated_page%20utm_team=Integrated). Evaluated on visionary innovation, security, sustainability, customer impact, and total cost of ownership, the [Cisco Desk Phone 9800 Series](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/phone-series/cisco-phone-9800-series.html) was recognized for transforming the desk phone from a single-purpose voice device into an intelligent workspace hub.
Cisco continues to advance the portfolio with innovations including the upcoming AI Translation Agent and the new Cisco [Wireless Phone 9821](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/phone-series/cisco-wireless-phone.html).
![Desk-Phone-9800-Series-aug-2026](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20626%20626%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
### Cisco named Company of the Year, Global Enterprise Desktop Phone, 2026.
Frost & Sullivan recognized Cisco for redefining enterprise telephony with the secure, sustainable, AI-powered [Cisco Desk Phone 9800 Series.](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/phone-series/cisco-phone-9800-series.html)
[Download Report](https://explore.webex.com/frost-sullivan-gated/frost-and-sullivan-d?utm_campaign=8265&utm_medium=website&utm_source=wdc_blogs&utm_term=download_report&utm_content=frost_and_sullivan_gated_page&utm_team=Integrated%20)
## Architecting the desk phone for modern work.
Enterprise desk phones were once designed and evaluated as fixed, single-purpose voice devices. But as hybrid work becomes standard, organizations need endpoints that can support flexible workspaces, personalized user experiences, and seamless cloud or on-premises operations.
The 9800 Series was built for this new reality. By bringing calling, workspace management, emergency notifications, and centralized administration into one architecture, Cisco helps IT teams reduce complexity while giving employees a more intuitive workplace experience.
## Five innovations that set Cisco apart, according to Frost & Sullivan.
#### 1. End-to-end security: hardware, privacy physical safety
The 9800 Series protects organizational privacy, infrastructure, and physical employee safety within a single hardware platform:
  * **Hardware-based root of trust:** The first desk phone portfolio featuring **TPM 2.0** as a dedicated hardware chipset, defending devices against supply-chain tampering and physical breach attempts.
  * **Private & secure communications: **Guarantees data privacy and network integrity through **TLS 1.3 encryption** , Secure Boot, image authentication, runtime defenses, and a built-in stateful firewall to prevent unauthorized access.
  * **Physical safety emergency escalation:** Includes a dedicated, high-visibility **[red Action Button](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=C7rzX3jSIvM)** directly on the device. IT teams can configure it for instant emergency calls, silent alerts, or custom facility dispatch with a single press.


#### 2. AI-powered audio & automated call capabilities
Instead of just routing voice calls, the 9800 Series actively enhances how teams communicate—removing background noise and language barriers while automatically capturing key action items and sending follow-up summaries straight to your inbox after the call:
  * **Distraction-free audio quality:** [Bidirectional AI noise suppression](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IMNfFYsycgc) filters out background chaos—like open-office chatter or home noise—on both ends of the line, keeping conversations clear and professional without requiring specialized headsets.
  * **Inclusion accessibility in real time:** Live closed captions and real-time transcription display spoken words directly on screen as people talk, helping hard-of-hearing team members stay fully engaged and easing comprehension in fast-moving discussions.
  * **Global team alignment:** Instant language translation breaks down cross-border communication barriers on the fly, allowing international teams and clients to connect naturally across different languages.


#### 3. Simpler deployment across cloud and on-premises environments
Deploying and maintaining thousands of devices can create significant administrative overhead. Cisco simplifies operations by bringing management into [Cisco Cloud Control](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BlyNwKSpxn4) for a consistent platform and streamlined provisioning.
  * **One operating system across deployment models:** PhoneOS supports both Cisco Unified Communications Manager and Webex Calling, helping organizations move between on-premises and cloud environments without complex firmware migrations.
  * **Zero-touch NFC provisioning:** IT teams can configure and onboard devices through Collaboration Control Hub using built-in NFC tags—without removing each phone from its box.
  * **Centralized management & observability:** Managed centrally through [Cisco Collaboration Control Hub](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/cross-platform/control-hub.html), within the single [Cisco Cloud Control](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BlyNwKSpxn4) platform, IT teams get one central place to manage all their Cisco tools, alongside built-in Cisco ThousandEyes intelligence for real-time diagnostics.


#### 4. Intelligent workspace management and desk reservation
The 9800 Series integrates with Cisco Spaces and Webex to make shared workspaces easier to [find, reserve, and use](https://video.cisco.com/detail/video/6384149070112).
> Cisco helps employees address a real and persistent frustration: arriving at an office only to waste time finding a suitable workspace, and solves it with a friction-free, technology-enabled workflow.”
> **— Chris Pennell, Industry Principal, Frost & Sullivan**
  * **At-a-glance availability:** Front and top LED indicators make workspace status easy to see.
  * **Flexible reservations:** Employees can reserve a desk in advance or directly from the phone when they arrive.
  * **Personalized sign-in:** Users can securely sign in from the phone display to access their settings and services.


#### 5. Measurable sustainability and greater ESG visibility
The 9800 Series is designed with sustainability in mind. Models use more than 74% post-consumer recycled plastics, include low-power modes, and are ENERGY STAR certified.
IT teams can also view estimated device-level carbon emissions in Collaboration Control Hub, helping organizations better understand and report on the environmental impact of their device estate.
## What’s next for the Cisco phone portfolio.
#### Real-time AI translation
Cisco has announced an upcoming AI Translation Agent for the 9800 Series, targeted for 1H CY27.
The feature will provide real-time speech translation during active calls, helping global teams and customer-facing employees communicate across language barriers without relying on separate third-party tools.

#### Introducing the Cisco Wireless Phone 9821
The Cisco [Wireless Phone 9821](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/phone-series/cisco-wireless-phone.html) extends the PhoneOS experience to deskless and frontline teams in industries such as healthcare, retail, and manufacturing.
Built for demanding work environments, the 9821 combines enterprise-grade voice, centralized management, mobility, and worker-safety features in a durable handheld device.
  * **Built for frontline environments:** Tactile, glove-friendly buttons, IP54 dust and water protection, and MIL-STD-810G durability.
  * **Designed for continuous shifts:** Swappable batteries, single and multi-device charging docks, and spare-battery options help keep teams connected.
  * **Reliable high-density connectivity:** Wi-Fi 6E supports low-latency voice performance in busy enterprise environments.
  * **Built-in safety features:** Push-to-talk, a dedicated emergency Action Button, and fall detection help support faster escalation when needed.


![frontline-workforce-promo](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20626%20626%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
### Cisco Phones, built for frontline work.
Explore the Cisco Phone 9800 Series and [Wireless Phone 9821](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/phone-series/cisco-wireless-phone.html) and learn how to keep frontline teams connected across healthcare, retail, education, and more.
[Watch On-Demand](https://www.webex.com/us/en/event-webinar/good-enough-connectivity-is-failing-frontline-teams-enable-reliable-communications.html)
#### **Frequently Asked Questions**
****Why did Frost & Sullivan name Cisco Company of the Year for enterprise desktop phones?****
Frost & Sullivan recognized Cisco for its innovation, market leadership, customer impact, and ability to address unmet enterprise needs. The report highlights how Cisco brings calling, hot desking, hardware-based security, sustainability, and centralized management together in one platform.
****How does the[Cisco Desk Phone 9800 Series](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/phone-series/cisco-phone-9800-series.html) simplify IT operations?****
The 9800 Series integrates with Collaboration Control Hub for centralized administration and ThousandEyes for network-path visibility and diagnostics. Together, these capabilities help IT teams monitor device health, identify performance issues, and resolve problems more proactively.
****Do organizations need new firmware when moving from on-premises to cloud calling?****
No. The 9800 Series uses PhoneOS to support both Cisco Unified Communications Manager and Webex Calling, helping organizations transition between on-premises and cloud deployments without a separate firmware migration.

#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.

# Zero Touch Provisioning: Instant Collaboration
On Aug 11, 2026Aug 11, 2026By [Rajesh Patwardhan](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/rpat/)2 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/zero-touch-provisioning-instant-collaboration) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/zero-touch-provisioning-instant-collaboration&title=Zero%20Touch%20Provisioning:%20Instant%20Collaboration) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/zero-touch-provisioning-instant-collaboration)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/zero-touch-provisioning-instant-collaboration/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/zero-touch-provisioning-instant-collaboration/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/workspace-ztp-1.jpg)
Imagine a workspace that self-configures, authenticates to the network automatically, and becomes instantly functional the moment it is plugged in. No activation codes, no manual scripts, and no back-and-forth verification. With Zero Touch Provisioning (ZTP) becoming generally available for all RoomOS 26 devices, this is now the reality.
By planning the onboarding process in Collaboration Control Hub, service owners can ensure that when an installer plugs in a device, the room comes to life immediately. It is the seamless transition from “ordered” to “operational” that modern IT teams require. 
## From Ordering to Operational: Eliminating Friction
Traditional deployments are often labor-intensive, requiring in-person setup, manual wizard navigation, and the entry of individual activation codes. For large-scale rollouts, this creates several bottlenecks:
  * Operational Complexity: Manually creating hundreds of activation codes and tracking device models is prone to error.
  * Communication Silos: Without a centralized plan, IT, partners, and on-site installers often struggle to stay aligned.
  * High Costs: The need for on-site technicians to visit every room to manually onboard devices are both time-consuming and expensive.


ZTP addresses these challenges by automating the entire lifecycle. In the Control Hub, you can now bulk-create workspaces, assign devices, and apply configuration templates before the hardware even arrives on-site. Once the plan is set, the installer simply connects the device, and the room becomes fully functional.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%202100%201443%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
## Secure Network Onboarding: Zero Touch, Zero Trust
A modern deployment must be secure by design. Today, provisioning unique certificates and managing six-month renewal cycles creates significant overhead. 
RoomOS ZTP simplifies this by supporting SCEP-based certificate enrollment with automated DHCP support. By integrating with [Cisco Identity Services Engine (ISE](https://www.cisco.com/c/en/us/products/security/identity-services-engine/ise-ds.html)), we enable not just “zero touch” but “zero trust” provisioning—ensuring every device is authenticated to the network before it ever receives its service configuration.
## The Power of the Cisco Ecosystem
When your collaboration hardware speaks the same language as your network, the experience is unparalleled. Using Cisco wired or wireless infrastructure, the network can automatically profile and authenticate devices via [ISE and Catalyst switches](https://www.cisco.com/site/us/en/products/networking/index.html). Furthermore, Cisco Wireless solutions can leverage location intelligence to verify that the correct device is being provisioned in the designated room, providing an extra layer of validation for the installer.
## Provisioning as the First Impression
Great user experiences begin long before the first meeting—they start the moment a device is ordered. With this first phase of ZTP, we are delivering the first of many productivity improvements for service owners. By leveraging the full power of the Cisco portfolio, we are redefining how workspaces are deployed.
Stay tuned as we continue to evolve this experience.
[You can find more information [here](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nujzhkab/Zero-Touch-Provisioning-\(ZTP\)-for-Board,-Desk,-and-Room-Series-devices).]

## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy

#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.

# How Estes Is Building Smarter Customer Care with Webex AI
On Aug 5, 2026Aug 5, 2026By [Krishna Tyagi](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/ktyagi/), [Jill Townsend](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/jill-townsend/)5 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/how-estes-is-building-smarter-customer-care-with-webex-ai) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/how-estes-is-building-smarter-customer-care-with-webex-ai&title=How%20Estes%20Is%20Building%20Smarter%20Customer%20Care%20with%20Webex%20AI) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/how-estes-is-building-smarter-customer-care-with-webex-ai)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/how-estes-is-building-smarter-customer-care-with-webex-ai/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/how-estes-is-building-smarter-customer-care-with-webex-ai/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/estes-truck-hero.jpg)
As North America’s largest privately held freight transportation company, Estes has earned a reputation for delivering high levels of customer care across all 50 states, Canada, Mexico, Puerto Rico, and the Caribbean. They consider every pickup request, tracking question, delivery instruction, and billing inquiry as an opportunity to keep freight moving and build customer confidence and loyalty.
Behind every Estes delivery is a contact center team focused on quality, consistency, coaching and agent productivity. Their 95-year-old company already had a strong quality process involving call reviews, agent report cards, performance tracking, and personalized coaching. But as with many large contact centers, their team faced a familiar challenge. Traditional quality management typically depends on manually assessing less than 2% of calls. Meanwhile, agents still must resolve their after-call effort in addition to focusing on customers.
The result is that valuable signals could be hidden inside everyday conversations. For example, what customers were asking, where agents needed support, which processes created friction, and where coaching could improve the next interaction.
Estes addressed the challenge through their close collaboration with Cisco. Together, the teams used [Webex Contact Center](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/contact-center.html), [](https://www.webex.com/content/dam/www/us/en/aag/webex-ai-am-at-a-glance-january2026.pdf)[Webex AI Quality Management](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/ai-workforce-engagement-management.html), and [Cisco AI Assistant](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/ai-assistant-for-contact-center.html) capabilities to expand visibility, strengthen coaching, reduce repetitive work, and create a more connected view of customer care.
> Partnering with Cisco has been a great experience. From the beginning, we had regular opportunities to share ideas, learn from each other, and work together to improve our operations. We thought we had a good quality audit program in our call center, but we realized we were only scratching the surface of what was possible. Our managers and agents now have real-time access to actionable insights, allowing us to coach more effectively, respond faster, and continuously improve the customer experience.”
> **— Jill Townsend, Director, Corporate Customer Experience, Estes Express Lines**
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
## Overcoming Quality Visibility Gaps 
Estes moved to [Webex Contact Center](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/contact-center.html) in 2023, creating the cloud foundation for a more modern customer care environment. The AI journey began with a focused question: Could AI help evaluate repeatable parts of more customer interactions while keeping supervisors in control of judgment?
Estes team members John Hoy, Chandler Fuller, Jill Townsend, Jason Amick, and Kyle Sais worked under the leadership of Vice President, Customer Experience and Innovation Carrie Estes Johnstone. Through early-access trials and design-partner sessions, they collaborated with Krishna Tyagi, Shivani Kolala, Mags Moran, Katie NicGabhann, Kirsten Esplin, Karen Sharpe Smith, and other Cisco contributors. The Cisco FDE-led engagement connected Estes directly with AI practitioners as well as Cisco product and engineering. The results turned real-world operational feedback into requirements and coordinated follow-up.
> Choosing the right technology partner is a critical business decision. Our experience with the Cisco AI Team has validated our choice at every turn. Cisco acts as a true strategic partner rather than a traditional software vendor. They took the time to understand our specific operational pain points before recommending solutions.”
> **— John Hoy, Network Engineer** , **Estes Express Lines**
## Building Quality Intelligence and Agent Assistance
Using [Webex AI Quality Management](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/ai-workforce-engagement-management.html), Estes began expanding from a narrow manual sample toward broader quality intelligence. At the same time, Webex AI Assistant capabilities helped gather intelligence for agents through post-call summaries, mid-call summaries, and auto wrap-up codes as well as visibility into customer sentiment. And the combination mattered. Supervisors gained a broader view of quality while agents were assisted with the work surrounding each interaction.
For the target lines of business, automation became the primary evaluation path for completed quality records. Automated QM activity grew by roughly 80% from March – June 2026.
AI could help evaluate procedural elements consistently, such as whether a required step was followed or whether a customer was placed on hold appropriately. Supervisors could then spend more time on the areas that require experience and context: whether the agent gave the right information, showed empathy, set realistic expectations, and helped move the customer forward.
That distinction is especially important in freight transportation. Customer care often depends on the service center’s practices and regional differences as well as the knowledge agents build from working across teams. Estes did not use AI to remove people from the process. Instead, it used AI to give supervisors better evidence and agents better support so they could focus on improving customer experience while supporting continued business growth.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
## Turning Insight Into Coaching and Agent Productivity 
As AI-generated quality data scaled, Estes began seeing encouraging signs of adoption across the supervisor workflow. Unique users visiting the Agent Performance grid increased 165% quarter over quarter, and the system recorded hundreds of coaching runs in the last quarter. 
Those signals matter because AI value is realized when people use the insight. Estes wanted quality teams to move faster from a score to the story behind it — all to identify the interactions, patterns, and agents that needed closer attention. Post-call summaries also became part of the agent’s workflow, helping agents document interactions more consistently, reduce repetitive after-call effort, and return their attention to the next customer.
## Measuring Impact
Wrap-up time declined from 15.3 seconds in March 2026 to 10.9 seconds in June 2026 — a reduction of about 28%. This is an early capacity signal; that less time in wrap-up means more time for customer-facing work, coaching, and higher-value follow-through.
## Connecting Quality, Agent Assistance, and Operations
Using near-real-time Topic Analytics, Estes identified more than 480 customer-contact topics, including delivery inquiries, dispatch-location issues, driver-location questions, dock-pickup requests, and pickup rescheduling. Supervisors could focus on the topics relevant to their queues and use near-real-time views when conditions change.The team also explored [Webex AI Workforce Management](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/ai-workforce-engagement-management.html), testing handle-time anomalies, adjustments, and occupancy assumptions.
> Cisco is on a very positive track towards being able to offer great QM/Auto-QM and WFM solutions for customers that may not require all the capabilities and resources that a call center with thousands of representatives would. They’re creating simpler solutions that are more manageable for supervisors and team leaders. Furthermore, it’s always a plus when you can have all your tools available to you in one suite or interface. It’s clear that Cisco has put lots of work into making a seamless and smooth experience for the end-user to navigate. While there are some enhancements that we’re still looking forward to, ‘I’m personally pleased with the products and am very appreciative of Cisco’s willingness to take and action feedback of real users.”
> **— Chandler Fuller, Analyst, Customer Experience** , **Estes Express Lines**
## A Foundation for What’s Next
Together, these results show AI moving from trial into the daily workflow — expanding quality visibility, supporting agents, and turning insight into action. Through the collaboration of Estes, Cisco, and our partner ePlus, the team is building a disciplined learning loop around adoption, productivity, capacity, and customer outcomes.
For Estes and Cisco, the next chapter is to continue the transformation across AI QM, AI Assistant, and AI Agent and validate the outcomes that matter most: supervisor time redirected from manual assembly to coaching, AI and human evaluation alignment, repeat-contact and resolution improvements, forecasting accuracy, plus the real-world impact of routing, language translation, and AI memory. The destination is a contact center where every interaction can help improve the next customer’s experience.

## Consent Manager

#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.
