# Eight years running! Cisco is named a Leader in the 2026 Gartner® Magic Quadrant™ for Unified Communications as a Service (UCaaS)
On Aug 3, 2026Aug 3, 2026By [Amit Barave](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/amitbarave/)7 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/eight-years-running-cisco-named-leader-2026-gartner-magic-quadrant-unified-communications-service-ucaas) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/eight-years-running-cisco-named-leader-2026-gartner-magic-quadrant-unified-communications-service-ucaas&title=Eight%20years%20running!%20Cisco%20is%20named%20a%20Leader%20in%20the%202026%20Gartner%C2%AE%20...) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/eight-years-running-cisco-named-leader-2026-gartner-magic-quadrant-unified-communications-service-ucaas)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/eight-years-running-cisco-named-leader-2026-gartner-magic-quadrant-unified-communications-service-ucaas/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/eight-years-running-cisco-named-leader-2026-gartner-magic-quadrant-unified-communications-service-ucaas/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/gartner-hero.jpg)
I am incredibly excited and proud to announce that Gartner has recognized **Cisco as a Leader inthe **[**2026 Gartner Magic Quadrant for our Unified Communications as a Service (UCaaS)**](https://www.webex.com/us/en/gp/gartner-magic-quadrant-ucaas.html) for the 8th consecutive year! To us, this positioning continues to be the result of our unwavering vision and proven ability to execute. We are truly honored by this recognition.
Webex has always been about connection — people, devices, tools. This past year, we’ve taken that further: connecting people with AI, and AI with AI. This recognition reflects what drives us every day: meeting customers where they are with innovation, security, and reliability — so they can focus on the work that matters most.
I believe these three areas are core to our continued acknowledgement:
#### 1. Delivering mission critical, high trust collaboration solutions.
Offering advanced reliability and resiliency, flexible deployment options (on-premises, hybrid, cloud), data sovereignty, and complete control and governance over AI.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%20830%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
  * **Reliability and resiliency are foundational.** With a 99.999% Webex Calling SLA and enhanced Survivability for Calling and Meetings, the mission keeps running regardless of network state — ensuring that an essential set of collaboration capabilities remain available if connectivity to the cloud is disrupted.
  * **Sovereignty is prioritized by design,** baked into the architecture from the ground up. Your organization stays in full control of its data and encryption, backed by a global footprint of more than 110 data centers, with residency in the US, Canada, the EU, Australia, New Zealand and the Pacific Islands, Saudi Arabia, and India, with Japan and the UK expansion underway.
  * Every organization has different control requirements, and Webex is built to meet them. Keys can live fully on premises in your own data center, in the cloud with you retaining exclusive control over who can access them, or anywhere in between. However your organization needs to manage them, no one outside your organization holds the keys to your conversations.
  * **Security is the engine of our AI strategy**. Cisco governs intelligence by default at every layer. Our Zero Trust architecture extends across every surface where collaboration happens — meetings, messages, recordings, whiteboards — with end-to-end encryption that’s tamper-proof today and quantum-safe for tomorrow.
  * **AI that runs without ever touching the cloud.**[Cisco AI PODs for Collaboration](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/cisco-ai-pods-collaboration-generally-available-delivering-secure-premises-ai/), now generally available, mean organizations can have AI-powered transcription and summarization running fully on-premises. Deployed against Cisco Meeting Server, AI PODs run on-premises or air-gapped with zero external connectivity. The data never leaves the defined boundary.
  * **Regulatory and corporate compliance requirements are met.** Webex Compliance Hub captures, retains, monitors, and governs communications across calling, meetings, messaging, and AI interactions. It simplifies eDiscovery, legal hold, compliance recording, and AI-powered surveillance, enabling organizations to reduce risk and respond more efficiently to audits and investigations.


#### 2. **Connected Intelligence Collaboration Platform**
We are seeing a fundamental shift in collaboration. AI is now embedded in every interaction — every call, every meeting, every chat. Webex Suite has evolved into a Connected Intelligence Platform, where Context, Conversations, and Outcomes are the engine driving workplace efficiency.
Today, context is continuous and everywhere. It doesn’t just flow across every interaction — it flows across People, Places, and Devices too. Every interaction makes the next one smarter. Webex is built on a platform that’s open, extensible, and AI-native — designed to support interoperability at scale while delivering the resiliency, security, flexibility, and observability our customers need.
We started with a standard AI Assistant to help users with daily tasks. In the past eleven months, we’ve seen a 9x increase in AI Assistant adoption in meetings. That tells us two things: it’s solving a real problem, and customers are building lasting habits around it.
We’ve also announced a suite of coordinated Productivity Agents that can handle routine work autonomously: capturing discussions, scheduling meetings, translating conversations in real time, and managing front-desk operations around the clock. Working in the background, these capabilities return time and attention to the work that actually matters.
Beyond productivity, we’re taking this further: Webex is transforming enterprise engagement by extending our comprehensive, secure AI Agent Studio across our entire Collaboration portfolio. The ability to build, deploy, and manage voice and digital multi-modal AI agents across any internal or external use case allows IT and admins to prioritize consistency.
And lastly, we have a robust Agentic Ecosystem of integration partners and MCPs, with integrations across Microsoft Copilot, Google Gemini, Anthropic Claude, Amazon Quick, and so much more — all governed through the same security architecture as every other Webex AI capability.
Taken together, this is what a Connected Intelligence Platform looks like in practice — not a single feature, but a system where context, agents, and partners work hand in hand to make every interaction better than the last.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%20943%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
#### 3. Delivering highly connected and unified management experiences across UCaaS, CCaaS, CPaaS, and Collaboration Hardware (Devices)
This approach delivers more innovative, faster, and more intuitive employee and customer experiences. From automating complex processes and enhancing customer journeys to providing actionable intelligence about Devices or office spaces, our unified innovations such as Cisco AI Assistant, and Collaboration Agents work effortlessly across hardware and software to deliver seamless experiences that don’t just help our customers innovate but make their lives and work easier.
**Everything comes together in Control Hub for IT**. Delivering exceptional user experiences is at the heart of what we do— but IT is the unsung hero who makes all of this possible in the real world. Webex is built to supercharge IT with [Collaboration Control Hub](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/cross-platform/control-hub.html), a single-pane-of-glass experience for managing Suite, Calling, Contact Center, and Collaboration Devices and providing a comprehensive, platform-wide approach to security and sustainability.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%201028%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
This year, we announced [Cisco Cloud Control](https://cloud.cisco.com/), Cisco’s unified platform for administration and AgenticOps. This AI-native management platform reimagines how organizations manage, operate, and optimize their IT infrastructure and gives teams one destination to access their complete Cisco estate from a secure, single sign-on experience.
[Control Hub](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/cross-platform/control-hub.html) is now part of the [Cisco Cloud Control](https://cloud.cisco.com/) experience. The powerful tools teams rely on to manage users, devices, workspaces, and collaboration services are now part of Cisco’s unified platform for AgenticOps, bringing collaboration management into a broader, AI-native operating experience.
#### 4. Unmatched Cloud Calling global availability.
Webex enterprise-grade cloud Calling delivers market-leading availability to over 19 million cloud calling users worldwide with three flexible options for PSTN connectivity and domestic calling in over 200 markets.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201671%20751%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
We continue to deliver enterprise calling and AI innovations to a [global customer base](https://www.webex.com/products/calling-global-availability.html), such as AI Receptionist, Cisco AI Assistant for Webex Calling, Collaboration Voice Agents and more.
Cisco also supports customers across both on-premises and cloud deployments, with [Cisco Unified Communications Manager](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/suite/enterprise-cloud-calling/CUCM.html) serving more than 30 million users worldwide, including organizations in government, defense, critical infrastructure, retail, manufacturing, healthcare, and financial services.
## Hardware meets software, built for how people actually work
Cisco’s next generation of collaboration devices makes it easy to video-enable every space, embeds intelligence into every device, and reimagines shared workspaces with desk reservation and modern hot desking. Our team has long been at the forefront of video collaboration, pioneering innovations that remove the impact of distance and [change how work gets done.](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZZLtqJVLuKc)
We’re able to provide that AI-driven intelligence because of our visionary NVIDIA partnership. The tight integration of Cisco’s world-class hardware, our leading conference room operating system RoomOS, and NVIDIA’s AI capabilities allows Cisco to deliver the industry’s most capable AI and machine learning platform, with up to 25x the AI capability of previous generations. It’s a depth of integration few UCaaS providers can match, because few build the room and the software running inside it.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%20830%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
## The power of one Cisco – the AI-powered platform advantage
What makes this possible is Cisco’s integrated, end-to-end solution. From the network to the software to the device, every layer is designed to work in harmony. This foundation enables us to embed intelligence deeply and securely across the entire collaboration experience, creating a seamless ecosystem where everything—and everyone—works better together.
![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201672%20830%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
## Join us at WebexOne!
Our commitment to delivering innovation and business outcomes remains stronger than ever. Expect even more advancements that improve employee and customer experience, with exciting announcements coming at [WebexOne, October 5-8](https://www.webexone.com/).
We’d love for you to join us—[register today for WebexOne](https://www.webexone.com/tickets.html?utm_campaign=8087%20utm_medium=website%20utm_source=webexone%20utm_term=reg_is_live%20utm_content=buy_tickets%20utm_team=WDC)—and see what’s next for Webex.
In closing, we’re so proud of what we’ve accomplished this year, but none of this is possible without our amazing customers and partners. Our innovation is driven by the needs of our customers, partners, and end users globally and in every industry. We truly owe this success to all of you!
__Gartner, Magic Quadrant for Unified Communications as a Service, Pankil Sheth, Megan Fernandez, Nitin Narang, 28 July 2026.__

#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.

# Tom Brady takes the stage at WebexOne 2026
On Jul 28, 2026Jul 27, 2026By [Aruna Ravichandran](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/arunaravichandran-2/)2 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/tom-brady-takes-the-stage-at-webexone-2026) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/tom-brady-takes-the-stage-at-webexone-2026&title=Tom%20Brady%20takes%20the%20stage%20at%20WebexOne%202026) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/tom-brady-takes-the-stage-at-webexone-2026)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/tom-brady-takes-the-stage-at-webexone-2026/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/tom-brady-takes-the-stage-at-webexone-2026/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/tom-brady-webexone-2026.jpg)
Every year, WebexOne brings together the people shaping the future of AI, collaboration, and customer experience. But the ideas that move an industry forward rarely come from inside that industry alone.
That’s what makes WebexOne different. Alongside the technology and the experts building it, we look to leaders whose experience spans far beyond any one field. Leaders who’ve navigated change, made hard calls under pressure, and built the kind of track record that travels — across roles, industries, and eras. Their lessons don’t stay contained to one industry. They apply to anyone leading through transformation.
**That’s why this year, we’re closing WebexOne with Tom Brady—7x Super Bowl Champion, Entrepreneur, and Philanthropist.**
Brady will join us in Austin for a keynote fireside chat on the power of collaboration, leadership, and resilience in moments of pressure and transformation. Drawing from experiences on the field and in the boardroom, we’ll dig into what it actually takes to build trust, adapt through adversity, and sustain excellence in an ever-changing, AI-powered world.
This conversation will be a defining moment of the event. It’s also a fitting way to close a week centered on one of the most important questions facing every organization today: how do we harness AI to transform the way we work, collaborate, and serve customers — without losing the trust and resilience that got us here in the first place?
The future of collaboration won’t be defined by AI alone. It will be shaped by how people and AI work together — to solve problems, make decisions, and create better outcomes. As AI moves from experimentation to everyday reality, the opportunity isn’t simply to automate work. It’s to fundamentally rethink how work gets done: how teams connect, how work flows across organizations, and how we create exceptional experiences for employees and customers alike.
That’s the conversation we’ll be having throughout WebexOne. Four days. 150+ breakout sessions. Hands-on training, product demos, and peer discussions. Attendees will get an early look at industry-first AI innovations — and hear directly from the customers, partners, Cisco leaders, and technical experts already putting those innovations to work.
WebexOne isn’t just where you’ll explore what’s possible. It’s where you’ll prepare for what’s next.
While the technologies we’ll explore throughout WebexOne point to where the future is headed, Brady’s perspective reminds us that lasting success has always depended on the same fundamentals: trust, resilience, adaptability, and the ability to bring people together around a common purpose. Those lessons are just as relevant in the age of AI as they’ve ever been.
Join us in Austin this October. Because the future of collaboration will be shaped by the conversations we have — and the people we learn from.

# The Voice of the Customer: Why Our 2026 TrustRadius Awards Belong to You
On Jun 22, 2026Jun 19, 2026By [Melody Leatherbury](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/meltang/)5 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/voice-of-the-customer-2026-trustradius-awards) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/voice-of-the-customer-2026-trustradius-awards&title=The%20Voice%20of%20the%20Customer:%20Why%20Our%202026%20TrustRadius%20Awards%20Belong%20...) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/voice-of-the-customer-2026-trustradius-awards)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/voice-of-the-customer-2026-trustradius-awards/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/innovation-ai/voice-of-the-customer-2026-trustradius-awards/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/trustradius-toprated-badge.jpg)
At Cisco, we talk a lot about customer success. But the true measure of our product’s value comes from the people who use our technology every day to connect teams, engage customers, and drive their businesses forward.
That’s why we are proud to announce that we have been honored with [**2026 TrustRadius Top Rated Awards**](https://solutions.trustradius.com/vendor-blog/2026-top-rated-award-winners/) for [Webex Suite](https://www.webex.com/suite/collaboration-suite.html), [Webex Customer Experience](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience.html) solutions, and [Cisco Collaboration Devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html).
These awards are especially meaningful because they’re based entirely on feedback from verified users. TrustRadius, a buyer intelligence platform acquired by HG Insights in 2025, has established itself as one of the most trusted sources for independent B2B technology reviews. Its Top Rated Awards reflect the experiences and outcomes customers achieve with the technologies they use every day.
## A Collaboration Portfolio Built for User Experience and Business Impact
The depth of this year’s recognition reflects the breadth of Cisco’s collaboration portfolio and the many ways organizations rely on our technologies to connect employees, engage customers, and create exceptional experiences.
### Webex Collaboration Software
  * [Webex Suite](https://www.webex.com/suite/collaboration-suite.html)
  * [Webex Meetings](https://www.webex.com/suite/meetings.html)
  * [Webex Calling](https://www.webex.com/suite/enterprise-cloud-calling.html)
  * [Webex Events](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/suite/events.html)
  * [Webex Webinars](https://www.webex.com/suite/webinar.html)
  * [Slido](https://www.slido.com/)


[**Webex Customer Experience Solutions**](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience.html)
  * [Webex Connect](https://cpaas.webex.com/products/webex-connect)


[**Cisco Collaboration Devices**](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html)
  * [Cisco Room Series](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/room-devices.html)
  * [Cisco Headsets](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/headsets.html)
  * [Cisco IP Phones](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/phone-series.html)


Together, our solutions earned a collective [trScore](https://www.trustradius.com/static/about-trustradius-scoring) of 8.9 across more than 219 verified reviews, securing wins in 14 categories including:
### Communication and Collaboration
  * UCaaS (Unified Communications as a Service)
  * Collaboration
  * CPaaS (Communications Platform as a Service)
  * VoIP (Voice over IP)
  * Web and Video Conferencing


### Events and Engagement
  * Event Management
  * Event Registration
  * Webinar
  * Virtual Events
  * Virtual Classroom
  * Employee Engagement


### Devices and Experiences
  * Conference Phones
  * Video Conferencing
  * Virtual Classrooms


## How the Scoring Works
TrustRadius has awarded Top Rated distinctions since 2016, selecting winners based on customer sentiment, satisfaction, and market relevance. You can explore the detailed [TrustRadius scoring methodology and criteria](https://www.trustradius.com/static/about-trustradius-scoring) to learn how these transparent, unbiased winners are determined.
## Why this Customer Recognition Matters
Awards are meaningful, but customer trust matters even more.
Because these accolades are driven entirely by feedback from verified users, this recognition represents something especially valuable: the voice of our customers. Your experiences, insights, and recommendations help shape our innovation and guide the evolution of our portfolio.
Being recognized across 14 categories—from UCaaS and Collaboration to Video Conferencing and Employee Engagement—underscores the impact Cisco collaboration solutions are having for organizations around the world. Whether enabling hybrid work, improving customer engagement, or delivering more immersive meeting experiences, we remain committed to helping teams do their best work.
To everyone who took the time to log onto TrustRadius, answer those detailed questions, and share your experiences—thank you. Your insights don’t just help other businesses make informed technology decisions; they directly shape our product roadmap. We read your reviews, we listen to your challenges, and we use your feedback to continuously improve.
## Hear Directly from Our Customers
The strongest endorsement comes from the people who use our solutions every day. Hear what verified users are saying about [Webex Suite](https://www.webex.com/suite/collaboration-suite.html), [Cisco Collaboration Devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html), and [Webex Customer Experience ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience.html)solutions—and why customers continue to trust Cisco:
> Webex is our primary collaboration platform. We use Webex Meetings for any and everything, ranging from collaboration between teammates for their daily work, to weekly team meetings, to large Town Hall type meetings. We have a very large Hybrid Workforce and Webex allows us to keep in touch with each other and ensures our caregivers can give our patients the best care possible.”
> [Shane Woolf, Lead Unified Communications Engineer, Community Health Network](https://www.trustradius.com/reviews/webex-meetings-2026-05-11-10-53-40)
> The Cisco Room series devices are the core of our conference rooms. They make the rooms simply and effortless to use. The Room Series devices also enable us to minimize or eliminate external peripherals. We have gone from needing to full rack of equipment for a room to just the single Cisco unit for most rooms.”
> [Verified reviewer, Professional in Corporate, Pharmaceuticals Company](https://www.trustradius.com/reviews/cisco-room-series-2026-05-11-09-07-58)
> In my organization, Cisco Headsets play a major role by allowing flexibility and mobility for my front desk co-workers, roles as receptionists, sales teams, NOC engineers, and warehouse staff are experiencing all the benefits using models such as Cisco Headsets 720 and 730. These models are capable of delivering high-quality audio while providing comfort and offering a long-lasting battery. Many of these areas no longer need to be configured to forward calls from their deskphone to their cellphones every time they leave the desk for some reason. This has fixed the problem of missing calls and reduced costs with PSTN calls.”
> [Leonardo Silva, Network Engineer, Logicalis](https://www.trustradius.com/reviews/cisco-headsets-2025-09-25-04-23-55)
> We previously relied on third party local phones and other applications, due to which, we faced inefficiencies and inconsistencies in client communication. Webex Calling helped us seamlessly integrate with our existing Cisco environment. Webex Calling enabled us to have local numbers and call routing across regions, helping us to maintain a local presence while operating globally.”
> [Nanda Chaitanya, Lead Analyst in Information Technology at Evalueserve](https://www.trustradius.com/reviews/webex-calling-2025-04-29-20-27-40)
> We use Webex Connect for customer communications and it has been awesome. The software ensures easy customer interaction and it is through this interactions that we better customer experience. It works across multiple channels including WhatsApp, SMS, Email and Voice and this ensures we interact with customers using their preferred channel. In addition, Webex Connect helps us automate and personalize customer journey and this leads to increased customer satisfaction which is good for business growth.”
> [Lagut Lehiany, Customer Relations Manager, IMPRIGRAPHIC](https://www.trustradius.com/reviews/webex-connect-2025-12-28-04-03-30)
At Webex, we’re proud to create products that inspire such gracious feedback in our user community.
## Celebrate Your Innovation with Us
We are incredibly honored by this recognition, but we know the best stories of innovation are the ones you are writing in your own organizations every day. We would love to shine a spotlight on your team’s hard work; if you have used our solutions to drive real business impact, we invite you to submit your story for a [2026 Webex Customer Award](https://www.webexone.com/awards.html). Then, come celebrate those wins with us in person at [WebexOne](https://www.webexone.com/), where we will be diving into the future of AI, collaboration, and the next wave of customer-led innovation. We can’t wait to see what you have achieved.

## Consent Manager
Your opt out preference signal is honored.
## Consent Manager
  * ### Your Privacy
  * ### Strictly Necessary Cookies
  * ### Performance Cookies
  * ### Targeting Cookies
  * ### Functional Cookies


#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.

# How Industry Leaders Turned Collaboration Into a Competitive Advantage
On Jul 17, 2026Jul 17, 2026By [Dik Whitten](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/dikwhitt/)8 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/how-industry-leaders-turned-collaboration-into-a-competitive-advantage) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/how-industry-leaders-turned-collaboration-into-a-competitive-advantage&title=How%20Industry%20Leaders%20Turned%20Collaboration%20Into%20a%20Competitive%20...) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/how-industry-leaders-turned-collaboration-into-a-competitive-advantage)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/how-industry-leaders-turned-collaboration-into-a-competitive-advantage/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/how-industry-leaders-turned-collaboration-into-a-competitive-advantage/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/top-brand-cisco-new-july.jpg)
We’re proud to highlight a group of forward-thinking organizations that are redefining how teams connect and collaborate. By leveraging the comprehensive [Webex Suite](https://www.webex.com/suite/collaboration-suite.html) alongside industry-leading [Cisco Collaboration Devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html), these customers have built seamless, inclusive hybrid environments that let employees do their best work from anywhere—and their commitment to a culture of productivity and well-being is setting a new standard for the future of work.
From global financial institutions like BNY, BBVA, and M&T Bank, to industry leaders such as Conagra Brands, AMD, Overhead Door, and Sutherland Global, to the team at NASA’s Kennedy Space Center, these organizations prove that the right technology is the heartbeat of a thriving culture. By standardizing on Webex and Cisco’s intelligent collaboration hardware, they’ve moved beyond simple connectivity to create immersive, data-driven spaces that close the gap between remote and in-office talent. Their transformation stories—marked by measurable efficiency gains, smarter space utilization, and a more engaged workforce—serve as a blueprint for any organization looking to thrive in the hybrid era.
### One Cloud Standard, 2,000+ Video Endpoints: How BNY Simplified Its Global Architecture While Elevating the Client Experience
BNY, a global financial services company, relies on [Cisco’s unified communications](https://www.webex.com/suite/collaboration-suite.html) and [video endpoint portfolio](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html) to maintain a secure, high-performance environment that meets the rigorous demands of the financial sector. BNY is currently driving a **strategic initiative to standardize their global fleet of over 2,000 video-enabled endpoints** on [RoomOS](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/roomos.html). By transitioning from on-premises call management to native cloud registration, BNY is significantly simplifying its architecture, reducing operational overhead, and ensuring a more predictable, high-quality user experience across its international offices.
This partnership is centered on BNY’s commitment to an exceptional client experience, where Cisco’s technology serves as the foundation for purposeful, frictionless meetings. Through the use of [Cisco Control Hub](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/cross-platform/control-hub.html), BNY’s IT team gains deep observability and manageability, allowing them to scale their operations and troubleshoot issues with greater precision. While BNY utilizes Microsoft Teams as its primary meeting platform, they leverage [Cisco’s Cloud Video Interop (CVI)](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/interoperability.html) and native device integrations to maintain superior video standards. Looking forward, BNY continues to explore advanced Cisco solutions, such as [Cisco Spaces](https://spaces.cisco.com/) and workspace analytics, to further digitize their workplace and drive data-informed decisions regarding real estate and meeting room utilization.
### From Fragmented Tech to Frictionless Collaboration: How Conagra Brands Cut Meeting Join Times 30%
[Conagra Brands](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/conagra-brands-updated-person-spaces-with-cisco-devices-webex-platform-improved-team-experience/?_gl=1*1d4ntkf*_gcl_au*MTIyNzQzODIuMTc4MTc0MDk0Nw..)—one of North America’s largest packaged-food companies, home to household brands like Healthy Choice, Marie Callender’s, and Slim Jim—faced a challenge familiar to any organization navigating hybrid work: a patchwork of complex, inconsistent meeting technologies that made collaboration harder than it needed to be. By standardizing its modernized Omaha office on [Cisco Collaboration Devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html) and the Webex platform, Conagra turned that friction into an advantage—**reducing average meeting join times by roughly 30% and giving employees one consistent experience whether they’re in the room or joining remotely.**
The renovated office replaced varied technologies with a single [Cisco Room Systems](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/room-devices.html) standard across every space—more than 20 reservable conference rooms and 10 video-enabled collaboration areas. Immersive setups built on [Cisco Board Pros](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/digital-whiteboards.html), [Room Vision PTZ cameras](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/cameras/cisco-room-vision-ptz-camera.html), and [Ceiling Microphone Pros](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/accessories/cisco-ceiling-microphone-pro.html), paired with dual-screen configurations, put remote colleagues on equal footing with in-room participants—closing the gap that so often divides hybrid teams.
The impact extends beyond the meeting itself. [Cisco Spaces](https://spaces.cisco.com/)-powered wayfinding helps employees navigate the open-concept workspace effortlessly, while [Cisco Control Hub](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/cross-platform/control-hub.html) gives IT real-time visibility into room utilization—turning guesswork into data-driven decisions about how space and resources are allocated. Most telling is the adoption: employee feedback has been overwhelmingly positive, with a minimal learning curve that let the investment pay off from day one. For Conagra, the result is a hybrid collaborative culture where seamless communication travels with employees across every location, backed by Cisco as a trusted partner in reliable, intuitive collaboration.

### Liftoff for Productivity: NASA’s Kennedy Space Center Retires Legacy PBX; Reduces IT Support Tickets 98%
The [NASA Kennedy Space Center Visitor Complex](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/nasas-kennedy-space-center-visitor-complex-reaches-new-heights-webex-cisco-collaboration-devices/?_gl=1*1d4ntkf*_gcl_au*MTIyNzQzODIuMTc4MTc0MDk0Nw..)—one of Central Florida’s most popular attractions, welcoming more than 1.5 million guests a year—modernized its communication infrastructure to keep pace with rising visitor numbers and operational demands with [Webex Calling](https://www.webex.com/suite/enterprise-cloud-calling.html) and [Cisco Collaboration Devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html). Led by IT Director Pedro Oñoro, the center replaced an aging PBX system with a cloud-based VoIP solution—enabling fast deployment across multiple locations and dramatically simpler troubleshooting through [Cisco Control Hub](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/cross-platform/control-hub.html).
The results speak to the scale of the shift: **internal communication improved, productivity climbed roughly** **85% and IT support tickets were reduced 98%**. Just as important, AI features like automated transcripts and closed captions made every meeting more accessible and inclusive across executive, operations, safety, and security teams.
Behind those numbers is a purpose-built collaboration ecosystem tailored to how each team works—[Cisco Desk Mini](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/desk-series/desk-mini.html) for executives, [Board Pro](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/digital-whiteboards.html) for teamwork, [Room Kit EQ](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/room-series/cisco-room-kit-eq.html) with AI-powered noise removal, and [Room Navigator](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/accessories/cisco-room-navigator-for-wall.html) for simple room control, complemented by Cisco phones and noise-canceling headsets for consistent, high-quality voice campus-wide.
### Overhead Door Turns 2,200 Devices and 2.8 Million Meeting Minutes Into a Future-Ready Workplace
[Overhead Door](https://www.webex.com/us/en/customers/overheaddoor.html)—the century-old American manufacturer that invented the upward-lifting garage door in 1921—made Cisco and Webex the cornerstone of its global collaboration strategy—and in doing so, reimagined how its people innovate. The company deployed more than 2,200 [Cisco collaboration devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html), including [Room Bars](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/room-devices/room-bars-and-kits.html) and [Board Pros](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/digital-whiteboards.html), to create a high-tech environment where engineers and designers can do their best work at a new innovation center.
The business impact reaches well beyond the hardware. By integrating [Webex Calling](https://www.webex.com/suite/enterprise-cloud-calling.html) and [Webex Contact Center](https://www.webex.com/us/en/products/customer-experience/contact-center.html), Overhead Door **simplified IT management while supporting** **2.8 million meeting minutes a year** and seamless global town halls. The strategic result: Webex has become the foundation of a more agile, productive, and future-ready organization.

### Proven at Scale: AMD Cuts Helpdesk Tickets 40% and Drives Room Utilization to 80-95%
AMD is a global semiconductor leader that views high-quality collaboration as a core discipline essential to its operations worldwide. As part of its ongoing workplace technology strategy, AMD sought to enhance the employee meeting experience by modernizing its global conference room ecosystem. The company recognized an opportunity to create a more consistent, reliable, and inclusive collaboration environment that would enable employees and leaders around the world to connect seamlessly, regardless of location. By investing in next-generation meeting technologies, AMD aimed to improve productivity, simplify user experiences, and support the fast-paced innovation that drives its business.
To address these challenges, AMD is executing a massive refresh of their global conference room fleet with [Cisco Collaboration Devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html), including [Room Kits](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/room-devices/room-bars-and-kits.html), [Board Pros](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/digital-whiteboards.html), and [Quad Cams](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/cameras/cisco-quad-camera.html), managed through [Cisco Control Hub](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/cross-platform/control-hub.html). This standardization has delivered **immediate, measurable value: helpdesk tickets have dropped by over 40%, and conference room utilization has surged to 80–95% as employees embrace the “one-button-to-join” simplicity.**
By leveraging Cisco’s agnostic endpoints and robust data analytics, AMD has not only streamlined global device management and reduced IT overhead but has also empowered facilities teams with actionable insights into room occupancy and environmental data, setting a new benchmark for an optimized, user-centric workplace.
This transformation reflects a principle that runs deep at AMD: serving as its own “customer zero.” AMD runs its global operations on AMD-powered technology, deploying enterprise-scale collaboration, compute, and AI within its own walls first—validating the very solutions it brings to market and turning each rollout into a proven playbook for customers. On that foundation, AMD IT layers its own AI to complement Cisco’s Control Hub telemetry, translating data on room utilization and device health into proactive action that anticipates support needs and automates routine IT workflows before they reach an employee. Delivered through a universal agent in Microsoft Teams and a suite of internal AI models running on AMD’s own compute, it marks a deliberate progression from assistive AI toward automation and autonomy—ensuring every AMD conference room benefits not only from best-in-class Cisco hardware, but from an AI-driven IT organization running end-to-end on AMD silicon.
### BBVA Standardizes 500+ Meeting Spaces to Power a 60/40 Hybrid Model
[BBVA](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/how-bbva-embraced-hybrid-work-with-cisco-devices/?_gl=1*bv1uw1*_gcl_au*MTIyNzQzODIuMTc4MTc0MDk0Nw..)—a global financial group serving more than 75 million customers across over 25 countries—**set out to make hybrid work—60% in-office, 40% remote—feel effortless across more than 500 diverse meeting spaces worldwide.** The challenge was to deliver one consistent, reliable, and secure experience across every room type while ensuring interoperability with external partners, all at the performance standard a leading financial organization requires.
The answer was a comprehensive suite of [Cisco Collaboration Devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html) and AI-powered [Room Systems](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices/room-devices.html) that automate noise cancellation and camera framing for a smoother, hands-free experience. With [Cisco Spaces](https://spaces.cisco.com/), the bank also gains data-driven insight into how rooms are used—enabling smarter space management and improved energy efficiency. The business value shows up at scale: **BBVA now hosts more than 800,000 meetings a year on the Webex Suite, powered by a consistent, scalable, agile communication environment** that lets the bank stay focused on core operations while Cisco continuously evolves the technology to meet what comes next.

### Sutherland Global Unifies 60,000 Users on a Single Platform to Scale Its Digital Transformation
[Sutherland Global](https://blog.webex.com/collaboration/how-webex-skyrocketed-sutherland-globals-efficiency/?_gl=1*bv1uw1*_gcl_au*MTIyNzQzODIuMTc4MTc0MDk0Nw..)—one of the world’s largest independent business process and digital transformation companies, with a workforce spanning some 20 countries—was held back by a fragmented collaboration environment. After the pandemic, overlapping tools hindered scalability, created inconsistent experiences, and drove up support complexity. The transformation required **migrating more than 60,000 users from Microsoft Teams to a unified platform** —while simultaneously moving from on-premises infrastructure to cloud-based [Webex Calling](https://www.webex.com/suite/enterprise-cloud-calling.html), all without disrupting business continuity or data integrity across a global workforce.
By standardizing on the full Webex Suite—[Meetings](https://www.webex.com/suite/meetings.html), [Messaging](https://www.webex.com/suite/messaging.html), [Calling](https://www.webex.com/suite/enterprise-cloud-calling.html), [Vidcast](https://vidcast.io/?utm_source=webex&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=webex-integration), and Webex Go—Sutherland enabled rapid cloud migration and a seamless experience across geographies and mobile devices. Using the [Cisco AI Assistant](https://www.webex.com/products/collaboration-ai.html) to automate manual tasks and tapping the Webex community for expert support, the company sharply reduced administrative burden, freed teams to work more efficiently, and achieved high adoption as it continues to scale.
### M&T Bank Connects 22,000 Employees Across 900 Branches With One Consistent Experience
M&T Bank, a nearly 170-year-old regional bank with over 900 branches across the Northeast and Mid-Atlantic, continues to invest in how its 22,000 employees connect and collaborate. As part of this focus, the bank is enhancing its meeting environments and digital tools to support a seamless experience across locations and formats.
By working with Cisco, M&T Bank has deployed an integrated suite of collaboration technologies—including [Webex](https://www.webex.com/suite/collaboration-suite.html), [Cisco Collaboration Devices](https://www.webex.com/us/en/devices.html), [Cisco Spaces](https://spaces.cisco.com/), and [Cisco Meraki Wireless](https://www.google.com/aclk?sa=L&ai=DChsSEwiOrNS8xdCVAxXWJUQIHWo8IjsYACICCAEQBBoCZHo&ae=2&co=1&ase=2&gclid=CjwKCAjwmdLSBhANEiwAkREMN7txVMYJbW4InPZi-oZtILGALmUsLDg-wNQ3_xKyQSf1_sV9FvX_lxoCk-cQAvD_BwE&cid=CAASWuRo74CVpvwvWkfbFoZWkZLNVNp6E0cr0uXAEMmp0f6yqHhTgD1yv2MC-Inr4AknRzuReW1WiOIggDi8b6WCdeGlc9KSdV_EAXw_x-rvJrY5PmLZO3V_N5Fi6g&cce=2&category=acrcp_v1_71&sig=AOD64_0KwsH2ypIQQFLOReB2iLtl2DxeDQ&q&nis=4&adurl). Together, these solutions create a consistent, user-friendly experience for employees, whether they are meeting in person, remotely, or across offices. The platform also provides greater visibility and flexibility. [Cisco Spaces](https://spaces.cisco.com/) helps employees easily find and reserve video-enabled rooms, while [Meraki](https://www.google.com/aclk?sa=L&ai=DChsSEwiOrNS8xdCVAxXWJUQIHWo8IjsYACICCAEQBBoCZHo&ae=2&co=1&ase=2&gclid=CjwKCAjwmdLSBhANEiwAkREMN7txVMYJbW4InPZi-oZtILGALmUsLDg-wNQ3_xKyQSf1_sV9FvX_lxoCk-cQAvD_BwE&cid=CAASWuRo74CVpvwvWkfbFoZWkZLNVNp6E0cr0uXAEMmp0f6yqHhTgD1yv2MC-Inr4AknRzuReW1WiOIggDi8b6WCdeGlc9KSdV_EAXw_x-rvJrY5PmLZO3V_N5Fi6g&cce=2&category=acrcp_v1_71&sig=AOD64_0KwsH2ypIQQFLOReB2iLtl2DxeDQ&q&nis=4&adurl) supports reliable connectivity for a mobile workforce. [Cisco Control Hub](https://www.webex.com/us/en/solutions/cross-platform/control-hub.html) allows teams to manage collaboration tools efficiently at scale.
These capabilities strengthen connectivity across M&T Bank’s footprint, support flexible work, and enhance how teams work together—reinforcing the bank’s ability to serve customers and communities with a relationship-driven approach.

## Defining the Hybrid Era, Not Just Adapting to It
These diverse success stories share one lesson: when organizations commit to a unified, intelligent collaboration strategy, they unlock new levels of productivity and engagement. By standardizing on Cisco’s Webex ecosystem and hardware, these industry leaders aren’t just adapting to the hybrid era—they’re defining it. As technology evolves, that commitment to seamless, data-driven connectivity will remain the cornerstone of a thriving, future-ready workforce.

[Workspaces](https://blog.webex.com/category/workspaces/)
# The Last Mile of AI Infrastructure Has Been Missing. Not Anymore.
On Jun 3, 2026Jun 2, 2026By [Espen Løberg](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/espenloberg/)6 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/last-mile-of-ai-infrastructure-has-been-missing-not-anymore) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/last-mile-of-ai-infrastructure-has-been-missing-not-anymore&title=The%20Last%20Mile%20of%20AI%20Infrastructure%20Has%20Been%20Missing.%20Not%20Anymore.) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/last-mile-of-ai-infrastructure-has-been-missing-not-anymore)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/last-mile-of-ai-infrastructure-has-been-missing-not-anymore/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/workspaces/last-mile-of-ai-infrastructure-has-been-missing-not-anymore/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/ai-infrastructure.jpg)
## **The Gap No One Is Talking About Honestly**
A global financial services firm rolled out AI-powered tools across their workforce. Adoption was climbing. Then their CIO walked into a conference room in Singapore and spent four minutes troubleshooting a display that would not connect. The tools were intelligent. The room was not.
That gap — between what AI promises and what physical infrastructure delivers — is the problem our entire devices portfolio was built to solve.
Employees working alongside intelligent agents expect the workplace to respond to them. Spaces that reconfigure around people. Devices that anticipate what is needed. Meanwhile IT is being asked to support more spaces, more workers, and more AI without more headcount. Facilities, real estate, and HR are each solving for their own piece of the puzzle with their own tools. Three teams. Three dashboards. One problem no one owns end to end.
Every seam between those systems is a place where the experience breaks down. Organizations investing heavily in AI find that if the physical infrastructure cannot support it, that investment stalls at the edge. The collaboration environment becomes the ceiling on everything else.
The workplaces getting this right made one decision: the technology has to work as a system. What sets Cisco apart is that we own every layer required to deliver that — the network, the device, the platform, and the intelligence. The switching infrastructure, the collaboration endpoints, the security architecture, the management platform, and the AI layer — integrated and accountable to a single architecture. Each of those layers exists elsewhere in the market. But not together. And Cisco uniquely provides every layer required.
## What Cisco Built For
The workplace where AI agents work autonomously alongside people — handling tasks, running workflows, and making decisions on behalf of the humans they serve — has two non-negotiable requirements. We engineered for both.
The first is environmental intelligence. Connectivity is the floor, not the ceiling. Every space aware. Every device a node on the network — generating insight, driving decisions, feeding the intelligence layer above it. Every worker, human or agent, supported by infrastructure that responds to them in real time. This is the difference between a room that has technology in it and a room that actually works.
The second is manageability at scale. Not by specialists dispatched to every room. Not by three separate platforms that share nothing. By a unified layer that makes the complexity invisible to the people who depend on it. The team managing a thousand rooms operates with the same confidence as the team managing ten.
These two requirements shaped every decision in the portfolio. The devices are not endpoints. They are the last mile — the physical layer where the network, the platform, and the intelligence stack become real for the people in the room. Built from scratch for the demands of autonomous AI at the edge.
## What Is Shipping Now
For decades, large collaboration spaces were a fragile patchwork of DSPs, extenders, and switchers from multiple vendors. Hard to deploy, impossible to manage at scale, and fundamentally incompatible with the kind of autonomous, edge-first AI the modern workplace requires. The Room Kit Pro G2 is where that architecture ends — and where a purpose-built infrastructure for intelligent work begins.
The Room Kit Pro G2 eliminates that complexity entirely. Built with a fourth-generation NVIDIA chipset delivering up to 25 times the AI processing power of its predecessor, it runs three components over a single cable: Room Vision PTZ camera, Ceiling Microphone Pro, and the compute core. One architecture, engineered for real-time spatial awareness, advanced noise removal, and people framing — all processed locally. No cloud round-trip. No latency. No sensitive data leaving the room.
The agents running on that architecture are native to the system — engineered in from the beginning. The Director Agent switches cameras, tracks speakers, and frames conversations autonomously across up to seven IP cameras and eight IP microphones. The Notetaker Agent captures and organizes action items without anyone touching a button. Translator breaks down language barriers in real time. These are not incremental improvements to a familiar product category. They represent a different category entirely — AI that acts, not AI that assists.
## Announcing the Board Pro G3
Every generation of the Board Pro has been guided by a simple principle: the space should serve the people in it. The Board Pro G3 takes that further than any previous generation.
It ships with expanded AVoIP camera and microphone support that brings visual collaboration to more spaces, and zero touch provisioning with wall, stand, and mobile mounting options. Setup that used to require specialists now takes minutes.
It is also the only MDEP (Microsoft Device Ecosystem Platform)-certified collaboration board — delivering a fully native MTR (Microsoft Teams Rooms) experience alongside Webex without compromise on either side. For organizations that have standardized on Microsoft but want the best hardware available, that certification is not a workaround. It is a first-class experience on both platforms simultaneously.
The deeper argument is about longevity. The Board Pro G3 is built on a platform designed to adopt AI models that do not exist yet. The compute, the security architecture, and the autonomous capability are all in place — no refresh required to access what comes next. Organizations buying the Board Pro G3 today are not buying a device with a three-year shelf life. They are buying a platform that evolves with the work.
## Interoperability Is Not a Compromise
One of the persistent assumptions in this space is that choosing the best devices means choosing a platform. It does not. For Microsoft environments, Cisco runs MTR on RoomOS with capabilities that go beyond the standard experience: 4K content sharing, AirPlay support, and native Webex meetings when needed. The result is an 80% quarter-over-quarter increase in MTR customers deploying more than a thousand devices.
95% of those customers manage everything through Control Hub — the same platform that eliminates the three-dashboard problem entirely [Source: Cisco Internal Data]. Facilities, IT, and real estate no longer need separate tools to understand what is happening across their spaces. One platform surfaces everything: space utilization, device health, and meeting quality, owned end to end. Over 2.3 million devices are connected today [Source: Cisco Internal Data]. Each one a data point on the network, generating insight — all surfaced through a single platform now extending into AWS, Microsoft Copilot, and other environments where IT teams already work.
Smart Diagnostics delivers one-click remediation before problems reach end users. Workspace Ranking tells the team managing a thousand rooms exactly where to look at 8:45 AM when a 9:00 AM meeting is at risk.
And this week, we are excited to announce that Workspace Advisor becomes generally available. The ability to convert any room into a digital twin, configure it remotely, and make changes without anyone setting foot in the space is not a convenience feature. It is a structural shift in how IT organizations operate at scale. The CIO in Singapore should never have to send someone to that room. Workspace Advisor means they no longer have to. For teams managing globally distributed real estate with constrained headcount, this is one of the most operationally significant releases we are making this week.
Also this week, Control Hub becomes part of Cisco Cloud Control — bringing cross-domain intelligence across networking, collaboration, and security into one management and troubleshooting experience. For IT teams managing complex, multi-platform environments without additional headcount, this changes what is operationally possible. Faster resolution. Fewer escalations. A management experience that finally matches the scale of the environments it is responsible for.
## Built Before the Market Asked for It
Every technology cycle arrives with new promises. The question worth asking is not whether those promises are ambitious — it is whether the infrastructure exists to deliver on them.
What I keep coming back to is this: the organizations that will get the most from AI are not the ones with the most sophisticated models. They are the ones that closed the gap between intelligence at the software layer and intelligence at the physical layer. That gap is where most AI investments quietly stall.
Cisco’s position in this market was not assembled in a product cycle. It was built over decades — the same architecture, the same conviction, every layer owned and accountable. The agentic workplace is not something we are racing toward. It is something we were already building when the conversation started.
That is what makes these announcements this week different. They are not just launches, but proof points — every layer in place, in market, and in customers’ hands. The last mile is finally here.

# The winning strategy for delivering exceptional customer experiences in the era of Connected Intelligence 
On Aug 6, 2026Aug 7, 2026By [Vinod Muthukrishnan](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/vinodmuthukrishnan/)6 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/connected-intelligence-customer-experience-strategy) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/connected-intelligence-customer-experience-strategy&title=The%20winning%20strategy%20for%20delivering%20exceptional%20customer%20...) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/connected-intelligence-customer-experience-strategy)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/connected-intelligence-customer-experience-strategy/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/connected-intelligence-customer-experience-strategy/ "Print")
![Intelligent Customer Experiences graphic showing connected interactions between people, people and AI, and AI systems.](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/intelligent-customer-experiences.png)
Customer experience is approaching an inflection point. As organizations add AI agents, automation, digital channels, and new sources of customer data, the experience risks becoming more fragmented—not less. Cisco’s answer is **Connected Intelligence** : an operating model that brings every customer interaction, every human and AI agent, workflow, and enterprise system together. 
Customers already feel the cost of disconnection. They repeat information as they move between channels. Automated interactions lose context. AI agents and human agents operate with different views of the customer. Behind the scenes, businesses must manage a growing web of models, applications, workflows, and security risks and deliver consistent customer experience every single time. 
AI alone will not solve this problem. Without the right foundation, it can magnify it. The next era of customer experience requires people, AI agents, channels, workflows, and enterprise systems to operate as one—carrying context across every interaction, coordinating the right response in real time, and earning the trust required to act on a customer’s behalf. 
This is the promise of Connected Intelligence, and it is the operating model Cisco is building for enterprise CX. Our approach and strategy is built on four connected pillars: an intelligent front door, context-driven orchestration, an extensible AI-native platform, and trust and secure by design. Together, they transform a collection of customer-experience technologies into one connected system that remembers, learns, acts, and improves with every interaction. 
![Agentic CX strategy showing four pillars—intelligent front door, context-driven orchestration, trusted and secure by design, and an extensible AI-native platform—supporting one experience, one platform, and One Cisco.](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201673%20940%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
##  **The Intelligent Front Door**
The first step toward intelligent CX is not the model. It is the moment of connection. 
Every customer interaction starts somewhere: a voice call, a chat, a message, a proactive outreach. The question is whether that entry point is intelligent enough to recognize intent, bring the right AI or human capability into the conversation at the right time, and preserve continuity as the customer moves across channels and modalities. 
In the era of Connected Intelligence, the front door is no longer just where the customer enters. It is where the experience begins to learn. An omnichannel, always-on, bidirectional engagement layer is the foundation on which everything else is built. Get this wrong and no amount of AI investment downstream will save the experience. 
##  **Context Driven Orchestration**
In legacy contact centers, routing was the control point. In agentic CX, the more strategic control point is context. That shift matters more than most people realize. 
Context is not the same as data. Data is a record. Context is a living signal, and the purpose of AI in customer experience is to use it the way humans do instinctively: to thread seemingly disconnected experiences together and respond in a way that feels completely natural. Think about how a person who knows you well operates. They don’t just remember facts about you. They know when to bring those facts up, and when not to. 
And this is where context gets difficult. The obvious signals are known: friction in a conversation, a repeated call, an unresolved issue. But the more powerful signals are the subtle ones. The slight hesitation before a customer answers. The pattern across five interactions that no single data point would reveal. The moment where the right response isn’t to solve a problem but to simply acknowledge it. 
That context is what powers intelligent orchestration: smarter routing, next best action, human handoff decisions, agent coaching, and self-learning AI agents that get better with every interaction. That is the intelligence we are building into the contact center. 
> _“As agents interact and pass information between one another, maintaining context becomes the difference between a system that feels intelligent and one that just feels automated. That’s why we’re investing heavily in a unified context and memory layer. It’s the foundation everything else gets built on.”_
> _Vinod Muthukrishnan_ , _VP & GM of Webex Customer Experience at Cisco_
##  **An Extensible AI Native Platform**
Connected Intelligence cannot thrive in a box of products. It needs a platform. Boxes are meant to be closed. Platforms are meant to be open. 
Integration was the standard of a previous era. Interoperability is the standard of this one. Systems that merely integrate pass data between silos. Systems that interoperate act as one. AI agents, human agents, customer context, channel workflows, CRM integrations, and enterprise systems all need to work securely and consistently across the customer lifecycle. When they  _do not_ , the customer feels the fragmentation. When they  _do_ , intelligence compounds and every interaction informs the next one. 
This is what we mean by an extensible AI-native platform: a secure, reliable, interoperable foundation for the AI and human workforce. Not a closed system that forces your experience into its architecture, but an open platform that connects to the tools, systems, and partners that matter to your business so intelligence can move across all of them. 
##  **Trusted and Secure by Design**
The AI era raises the stakes for trust. It is not enough for AI to answer quickly or personalize effectively. Enterprises need AI-powered CX that can be secured, observed, managed, and scaled with confidence. 
It has never been easier to build an AI agent. It has never been harder to make one enterprise grade. At Cisco, security is not a feature we add on. It is foundational to everything we build, and that heritage runs through every layer of what we bring to the contact center. 
An AI agent that can take action is also an AI agent that can take the wrong action. It can expose data it should not have accessed. It can make a commitment the business cannot honor. It can be manipulated into doing something it was never designed to do. These are not edge cases. They are the new reality of agentic AI, and they require an architectural response, not a software patch. 
![Graphic connecting conversations, actions, and autonomy alongside the statement, “Autonomy without accountability is liability.](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%201742%20903%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
This is where Cisco is fundamentally differentiated. By integrating capabilities from Splunk’s observability with native AI Defense, we deliver real-time detection, anomaly monitoring, and native AI guardrails across the entire stack. 
Enterprise-grade security, manageability, and observability are not constraints on innovation. They are what makes innovation sustainable at scale. 
##  **One Experience. One Platform. One Cisco.**
![Customer service agent using Webex Contact Center with Cisco AI Assistant displaying a live call transcript and customer insights.](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20viewBox=%220%200%20936%20493%22%3E%3C/svg%3E)
Customers do not experience a business as a collection of channels, systems, and teams. They experience one company. They know when that company recognizes them, remembers the last conversation, and gets them to the right help—and they feel the disconnect when it does not. 
Connected Intelligence closes that gap. It starts with an intelligent front door that understands why the customer is there. It carries context throughout the journey, connects people and AI across an open platform, and builds in the security and trust every interaction depends on. 
One platform does not mean one product. It means the people, AI agents, applications, and systems behind the customer experience can work as one. It means giving businesses a simpler way to deploy, secure, manage, and scale AI from metal to model. The goal is cohesion, not complexity. 
And One Cisco means bringing the full strength of Cisco to that challenge—connecting everything from the underlying network to the applications where customers and employees engage. That is the opportunity in front of us: to move beyond disconnected moments and create experiences that feel continuous, personal, and trusted. 
**That is Connected Intelligence—every interaction connected to what came before, and making what comes next better.**

#### Your Privacy
When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. From the list on left, please choose whether this site may use Performance and/or Targeting Cookies. By selecting Strictly Necessary Cookies only, you are requesting Cisco not to sell or share your personal data. Note, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience on the site and the services we are able to offer.
#### Strictly Necessary Cookies
Always Active
These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.
Cookies Details
#### Performance Cookies
Performance Cookies
These cookies provide metrics related to the performance and usability of our site. They are primarily focused on gathering information about how you interact with our site, including: page load times, response times, error messages, and allowing a replay of a visitor’s interactions with our site, which enables us to review and analyze visitor behavior, helping to improve site usability and functionality. These cookies also allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site and will not be able to monitor its performance.
Cookies Details
#### Targeting Cookies
Targeting Cookies
These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.
Cookies Details
#### Functional Cookies
Functional Cookies
These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.

# How GetixHealth enhanced operations and improved attrition rates with Webex
On Jul 9, 2026Aug 12, 2026By [Melody Leatherbury](https://blog.webex.com/contributors/meltang/)6 Min Read
[ ](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?url=https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/getixhealth-enhanced-operations-improved-attrition-rates-with-webex) [ ](https://www.linkedin.com/shareArticle?mini=true&url=https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/getixhealth-enhanced-operations-improved-attrition-rates-with-webex&title=How%C2%A0GetixHealth%C2%A0enhanced%20operations%20and%20improved%20attrition%20rates%20...) [ ](https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/getixhealth-enhanced-operations-improved-attrition-rates-with-webex)
[ ](https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/getixhealth-enhanced-operations-improved-attrition-rates-with-webex/ "Copy Link") [ ](https://blog.webex.com/customer-experience/getixhealth-enhanced-operations-improved-attrition-rates-with-webex/ "Print")
![](https://blog.webex.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/getixhealth-houston-lobby.jpg)
Founded in 1992, GetixHealth is a leading revenue cycle management partner to healthcare organizations across the country. The company serves over 400 hospitals and 10,000 physicians with services that include patient access, eligibility & enrollment, insurance resolution, and self-pay patient billing.
GetixHealth is focused on streamlining communication and financial management to ensure that providers can prioritize what’s most important: serving patients with exceptional care. In total, the team has served over one hundred million patient transactions, and manages over $12B in A/R for its clients.
With more than 2,000 employees across 11 global offices, including two centers in India, the company needed a reliable way to support rapid growth without sacrificing employee experience or service quality. It also needed a modern infrastructure that could scale with its long-term ambitions.
